We use this controlled telemetry to evaluate a machine-learning-based dependency recovery pipeline that formulates the conversion of continuous traces into binary event indicators as a significance-detection problem. Experimental evaluation shows that the proposed pipeline reliably recovers the latent dependency structure from noisy continuous traces when the signal is sufficiently separated from background variation, while highlighting threshold calibration as the key factor controlling event-detection quality. These results constitute a foundational step toward interpretable dependency learning for adaptive AI-RAN control systems.

</details>


### [224] [PC Layer: Polynomial Weight Preconditioning for Improving LLM Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06470)
*Senmiao Wang,Tiantian Fang,Haoran Zhang,Yushun Zhang,Kunxiang Zhao,Alex Schwing,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过多项式预条件子对权重矩阵进行参数化的预条件层（PC层），能在LLM训练中稳定权重条件数，训练后可合并回原始架构且无推理开销，在Llama-1B预训练中优于标准Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模语言模型（LLM）训练过程中权重矩阵条件数不稳定导致的优化困难问题。

Method: 设计预条件层（PC层），通过低阶多项式预条件子重塑权重矩阵的奇异值谱，实现对权重条件的稳定控制。该方法支持AdamW和Muon优化器，且训练后可将预条件权重合并回原架构。

Result: 在Llama-1B预训练任务中，使用PC层的Transformer在AdamW和Muon两种优化器下均优于标准Transformer。

Conclusion: 理论证明了在深层线性网络中，均匀有界化每层的奇异值能确保梯度下降几何收敛到全局最小值，验证了谱控制原则的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a preconditioning (PC) layer, a weight parameterization via polynomial preconditioner that ensures stable weight conditioning throughout LLM training. The PC module reshapes the singular-value spectrum of weight matrices via low-degree polynomial preconditioning. After training, the preconditioned weights can be merged back into the original architecture, incurring no inference overhead. We demonstrate the advantage of the proposed PC layer over standard transformers in Llama-1B pre-training, for both the AdamW and Muon optimizers. Theoretically, we justify this spectrum-control principle by proving that uniformly bounding each layer's singular values ensures geometric convergence of gradient descent to global minima, for certain deep linear networks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Empath-aln/PC-layer.

</details>


### [225] [RREDCoT: Segment-Level Reward Redistribution for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06475)
*Mykyta Ielanskyi,Kajetan Schweighofer,Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RREDCoT方法，利用模型自身对CoT轨迹进行奖励重新分配，解决GRPO等强化学习方法在推理语言模型训练中因延迟奖励导致的高方差问题，无需额外生成即可近似最优奖励分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GRPO的推理语言模型强化学习微调面临延迟奖励问题——只有在完整CoT轨迹结束后才能分配奖励，这相当于蒙特卡洛方法，存在高方差问题。虽然可以通过信用分配（奖励重新分配）来缓解，但蒙特卡洛采样在长上下文高粒度场景下的计算开销使其不适用于训练时的信用分配。

Method: RREDCoT利用模型自身对CoT轨迹进行奖励重新分配，无需额外生成即可近似最优奖励分配。具体包括：利用模型自身进行状态价值估计，以及对CoT轨迹进行分段处理以实现细粒度的信用分配。

Result: 与蒙特卡洛采样和多种归因方法相比，RREDCoT在奖励重新分配任务上展现出优势。此外，论文还分析了CoT轨迹分段和状态价值估计等关键因素对重新分配效果的影响。

Conclusion: RREDCoT提供了一种无需额外生成开销的奖励重新分配方案，能够有效缓解GRPO方法中的高方差问题，为推理语言模型的强化学习训练提供了更高效的信用分配机制。

Abstract: Recent advancements in reasoning language models have been driven by Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning. Most often, these rely on the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) algorithm or modifications thereof to steer the models to produce Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces. The final answer can only be verified, and the reward assigned, after the CoT trace is complete, making it a delayed reward problem. GRPO and its modifications correspond to Monte Carlo methods in standard RL, which are known to suffer from high variance. A possible solution to this problem is the redistribution of rewards through credit assignment, where segments of the CoT trace that are important for arriving at the desirable solution are emphasized by assigning a higher reward. While Monte Carlo sampling can be used to provide an unbiased estimate of intermediate state values, its computational overhead makes it unsuitable for train-time credit assignment in long contexts at high granularity. We introduce RREDCoT (Reward REDistribution for Chain of Thoughts), which utilizes the model itself to approximate the optimal reward redistribution without additional generation. We investigate the advantages of our method compared to MC sampling and several attribution methods. We further analyze several aspects relevant to the construction of the redistribution such as segmentation of CoT traces and state value estimation.

</details>


### [226] [Pretraining Recurrent Networks without Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06479)
*Akarsh Kumar,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出监督记忆训练（SMT）方法，通过将RNN训练转化为对一步记忆转移标签的监督学习，避免了循环信用分配问题，实现时间并行训练和稳定O(1)梯度路径，在语言建模等任务中优于BPTT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BPTT训练RNN存在时间顺序限制导致的并行性差、长期依赖梯度消失/爆炸等问题，需要一种能并行训练且有效捕捉长距离依赖的RNN训练方法。

Method: SMT通过Transformer编码器基于预测状态目标生成一步记忆转移标签$(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$，将RNN训练简化为对这些标签的监督学习，从而解耦"记住什么"与"如何更新记忆"，实现无需展开RNN的时间并行训练。

Result: 在语言建模和像素序列建模等任务上，SMT预训练多种RNN架构的表现优于BPTT；实现了任意两个token间稳定的O(1)长度梯度路径。

Conclusion: SMT使非线性RNN能更好地捕捉长距离依赖并实现并行训练，有望推动构建时间抽象模型的规模化发展。

Abstract: Training recurrent neural networks (RNNs) requires assigning credit across long sequences of computations. Standard backpropagation through time (BPTT) addresses this problem poorly: it is sequential in time, limiting parallelism, and suffers from vanishing or exploding gradients, making long-range associations difficult to learn. We propose Supervised Memory Training (SMT), a method for training nonlinear RNNs that sidesteps recurrent credit propagation entirely by reducing RNN training to supervised learning on one-step memory transition labels $(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$. SMT acquires these memory labels by training a Transformer-based encoder on a predictive state objective--retaining only information from the past necessary to predict the future. By decoupling what to remember from how to update memory, SMT enables time-parallel RNN training with a stable $O(1)$ length gradient path between any two tokens--without ever unrolling the RNN. We find that SMT outperforms BPTT when pretraining various RNN architectures on tasks like language modeling and pixel sequence modeling. SMT enables nonlinear RNNs to better capture long-range dependencies and train in parallel, potentially unlocking the scaling of models that build temporal abstractions of past experience.

</details>


### [227] [Regret Minimization with Adaptive Opponents in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06486)
*Mingyang Liu,Asuman Ozdaglar,Tiancheng Yu,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于自适应对手的重复博弈新遗憾度量——重复策略遗憾（RP-Regret），并研究了其最小化条件与算法，证明在所有玩家最小化该遗憾时可学习子博弈完美均衡，实验表明能获得更高合作效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准外部遗憾无法捕捉自适应对手的博弈特性，需要一种能反映玩家反事实推理的博弈论度量来刻画重复博弈中的学习与均衡。

Method: 定义RP-Regret，识别其子线性增长的必要条件（策略比较器变分、记忆约束），针对非凸优化挑战提出三种算法：基于优化oracle的方法、线性化凸替代最小化方法、对手策略缓慢变化时的直接最小化方法。

Result: 当所有玩家最小化RP-Regret或其线性化变体时，可学习到某些子博弈完美均衡；实验表明在Stag-Hunt等博弈中可获得更高合作效用。

Conclusion: RP-Regret作为原生重复博弈遗憾度量，支持更强的比较器和更少约束的对手，为自适应环境下的在线学习与博弈均衡提供了新框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study regret minimization in repeated games with \emph{adaptive} opponents who can respond based on histories of play. The standard metric of \emph{external regret} in online learning is known to fail to capture such adaptivity. To account for players' counterfactual reasoning, we introduce {\tt Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)}, a game-theoretic metric that measures the difference between the \emph{realized} and the \emph{best-in-hindsight} accumulated utility when all players can \emph{respond} to the history of play. Compared to existing regret notions in this setting, ours is native to repeated game playing, enabling stronger comparators and opponents with fewer constraints, while maintaining the possibility of finding better equilibria when all players minimize it. We first identify necessary conditions for obtaining {\tt RP-Regret} sublinear in time, on the variation of the player's comparator strategies in the regret definition and on the memories of both the comparator and opponents' strategies. We then study additional conditions and provable algorithms to minimize {\tt RP-Regret}, which is by definition \emph{non-convex} in the strategy space. To address this challenge, we propose three algorithms: (i) one based on an optimization oracle, as assumed in some prior work in online non-convex learning; (ii) one that minimizes a convex and \emph{linearized} surrogate of {\tt RP-Regret} at each iteration; (iii) one that directly minimizes {\tt RP-Regret} when opponents change strategies slowly. Furthermore, when all players can run algorithms to minimize the {\tt RP-Regret} (or its linearized variant), certain subgame perfect equilibria of the repeated game can be learned. We also provide experiments showing that minimizing our regret notions can lead to more cooperative solutions with higher utility in games such as Stag-Hunt.

</details>


### [228] [TailLoR: Protecting Principal Components in Parameter-Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06494)
*Marius Dragoi,Ioana Pintilie,Alexandra Dragomir,Antonio Barbalau,Florin Brad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TailLoR利用预训练权重的奇异基作为固定参考系，通过软谱惩罚抑制主奇异方向上的更新干扰，将细粒度适应导向长尾谱坐标，实现参数高效的持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于谱分解的参数高效微调方法在持续学习中已有进展，但现有方法在减少任务间干扰和实现灵活适应方面仍存在不足。

Method: 利用预训练权重的奇异基U和V作为固定参考框架，学习对奇异值矩阵的低秩更新；引入软谱惩罚，抑制与主导奇异方向对齐的更新，将细粒度适应路由到高灵活性的长尾谱坐标。

Result: 软谱惩罚有效减少了任务间干扰，同时将适应能力导向长尾谱坐标，提升了细粒度适应性。

Conclusion: TailLoR通过谱坐标路由和软惩罚机制，在持续学习中实现了更优的干扰减少与适应灵活性平衡。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient finetuning methods based on spectral decomposition have enabled progress in Continual Learning. In this paper we introduce TailLoR, which utilizes the singular bases U and V of the pre-trained weights as a fixed reference frame to learn a low-rank update applied to the singular value matrix. A soft spectral penalty discourages updates aligned with dominant singular directions, reducing interference while routing fine-grained adaptation into the highly flexible, long-tail spectral coordinates.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [Gradient descent at the Edge of Stability: free energy model and kinetic description of the two-layer network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05326)
*Antonin Chodron de Courcel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了梯度下降在“稳定边缘”（Edge of Stability） regime下的动力学，提出一个连续时间有效模型，通过平均轨迹与快速振荡的时间平均协方差来追踪损失和锐度的持续振荡。分析表明，该不稳定 regime 中的自然监控量是有效自由能，它结合了原始风险泛函与曲率相关的“熵”项。模型能够追踪振荡包络线，即使其动力学与平均权重在相似时间尺度上演化。针对宽两层神经网络在稳定非衰减振荡下的优化，推导出平均场极限，得到描述权重及其波动联合分布的新型动力学方程，该方程可解释为宏观自由能的 Wasserstein-2 梯度流。在矩阵分解和深度学习任务（CIFAR-10）上提供了数值证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解梯度下降在较大学习率导致损失和锐度持续振荡的“稳定边缘” regime 下的动力学行为，并建立有效的理论框架来捕捉这种不稳定训练动态。

Method: 提出连续时间有效模型，追踪平均轨迹与快速振荡的时间平均协方差；引入有效自由能（原始风险+曲率相关熵项）；针对宽两层神经网络推导平均场极限，得到权重-波动联合分布的动力学方程，并证明其为 Wasserstein-2 梯度流。

Result: 模型能准确追踪振荡包络线，即使其动力学与平均权重时间尺度相近；在矩阵分解和 CIFAR-10 深度学习任务上验证了模型的准确性及有效自由能的预测能力。

Conclusion: 在稳定边缘 regime 下，有效自由能是监控训练动态的自然量；平均场极限导出的动力学方程揭示了该不稳定训练过程的宏观自由能景观，为理解大规模神经网络在非稳定振荡下的训练行为提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of gradient descent in the Edge of Stability regime, where the learning rate is large enough to induce persistent oscillations in the loss and the sharpness. We propose a continuous-time effective model that tracks the evolution of the average trajectory coupled with the time-averaged covariance of its fast oscillations. Our analysis reveals that the natural quantity to monitor in such unstable regimes is an effective free energy, which combines the original risk functional with a curvature-related "entropic" term. Our model allows us to track the envelope of the oscillations even in situations where its dynamics evolve on similar timescales as the averaged weights. Otherwise stated, we can track the spikes that occur during the training of some neural network architectures. For wide two-layer neural networks optimized under stable non-vanishing oscillations, we derive a mean-field limit that results in a novel kinetic equation describing the joint distribution of weights and their fluctuations. We show that this equation can be interpreted as a Wasserstein-2 gradient flow of a macroscopic free energy. Finally, we provide numerical evidence on matrix factorization and deep learning tasks (CIFAR-10) to demonstrate the model's accuracy in capturing the envelope of the oscillations and the predictive power of the effective free energy.

</details>


### [230] [Learning Implicit Feasibility Constraints for Real-World Routing and Scheduling: Application to Log Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05353)
*Abdelhakim Abdellaoui,Ayoub Boufous,Issmail El Hallaoui,Loubna Benabbou,Francois Aubé,Mouloud Amazouz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的约束学习框架，通过训练GNN、决策树和线性回归三个互补预测器，并利用堆叠机制将它们嵌入MILP中，使求解器能够内生选择最可靠的预测器，从而在物流调度问题中自动学习隐藏的操作约束，减少人工调整，且保持可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际车辆路径与调度问题中，操作规则频繁变化（季节性/永久性），而现有优化工具依赖固定硬编码约束，更新需要建模和运筹学专业技能，规划人员难以胜任，导致计划常需人工调整。因此需要一种能从历史数据中自动学习隐藏约束并嵌入优化模型的方法。

Method: 基于历史执行数据，训练三个互补预测器（GNN、决策树、线性回归），通过线性化约束将其各自嵌入MILP；进一步提出堆叠机制，将三个预测器组合成单一增强优化问题（AOP），让求解器内生选择每个决策的最可靠预测器。

Result: 在工业数据上，每个单独预测器均能改善可行性，但堆叠嵌入始终取得最低的目标退化：在未见实例上满足操作规则且退化最小，无需先验知识即可为每个决策选择最合适的预测器，减少日常人工调整量，同时保持计算可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架使优化工具能够适应不断变化的实际操作规则，而无需重复人工建模，为物流调度等领域提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world vehicle routing and scheduling problems involve complex operational rules and feasibility constraints typically formulated as mixed-integer linear programs (MILP). However, optimization tools are built around a fixed set of hard-coded constraints, while in practice this set evolves as new rules or preferences emerge, seasonally or permanently. Updating it requires modeling and operations research skills that planners rarely have, so generated plans are routinely adjusted by hand based on practical knowledge. Building on recent work that uses machine learning to recover such hidden constraints, we propose a data-driven constraint-learning approach that trains three complementary predictors, a Graph Neural Network (GNN), a decision tree, and a linear regression, on historical execution data from a log-truck routing and scheduling problem ($\mathcal{LTRSP}$), and embeds each inside a MILP through linearized constraints. We further introduce a stacking mechanism that combines all three within a single augmented optimization problem (AOP), letting the solver endogenously select the most reliable predictor for each decision. On real-world industrial data, each predictor already improves feasibility, but the stacked embedding consistently achieves the lowest objective degradation: it (i)~satisfies the operational rules on unseen instances with smaller degradation than any single-model variant, (ii)~picks the most appropriate predictor per decision without prior knowledge of the rule's nature, and (iii)~reduces daily manual adjustment effort while remaining tractable for daily use. Beyond this application, the framework enables optimization tools that adapt to evolving practice without recurrent manual remodeling.

</details>


### [231] [Bayesian Equilibria of Heterogeneous Non-Atomic Routing Games with Private Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05424)
*Alexia Ambrogio,Leonardo Cianfanelli,Giacomo Como,Paolo Frasca*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了一个共享交通网络中协调车队与自私用户共存的贝叶斯路由博弈，证明在有限状态和消息集下贝叶斯均衡的存在性与唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实交通系统中同时存在可控的协调车队和不可控的自私用户，且两者都基于不完全信息（如随机路况、私有信号）做路由决策，现有模型缺乏对这种异构信息结构下博弈均衡的理论分析。

Method: 建立非原子贝叶斯路由博弈模型，假设世界状态和私有消息集均为有限集，利用博弈论和不动点理论证明贝叶斯均衡的存在性与唯一性。

Result: 在有限状态和有限消息集的条件下，证明了该贝叶斯路由博弈存在唯一的贝叶斯均衡。

Conclusion: 该理论框架为分析具有异构部分信息的混合交通流路由决策提供了严谨的均衡理论基础。

Abstract: We study non-atomic Bayesian routing games whereby a transportation network is shared by two types of traffic: a coordinated fleet and a mass of selfish users. The links in the network are characterized by travel time functions that depend both on the aggregate flow on the link and on a random state of the world $W$ that, in general, is not directly observable. Rather, we assume that both the fleet coordinator and the selfish users know the prior distribution of $W$, observe (different) private messages that are correlated with $W$ and possibly among themselves, and make routing decisions based on such heterogeneous partial information. Under the assumption that both the state of the world and the private message sets are finite, we prove the existence and uniqueness of a Bayesian equilibrium for the ensuing Bayesian routing game.

</details>


### [232] [Optimal experimental design for passive imaging source problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05490)
*Christian Aarset,Thorsten Hohage,Georg Stadler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对被动成像中的最优实验设计（OED）问题，提出了一种基于双层次低秩近似的A-最优设计方法，有效解决了相关数据导致观测维度二次增长的计算瓶颈，并通过离线-在线两阶段策略避免了重复求解偏微分方程，数值实验表明该方法可高效扩展至大规模问题且显著优于随机传感器布置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动成像中，相关数据的使用导致观测维度随传感器数量二次增长，这使得最优设计的计算难度加剧。现有方法因需要反复求解PDE而面临计算瓶颈，亟需一种高效的可扩展方法。

Method: 采用贝叶斯逆问题框架，针对Helmholtz方程控制的空间不相关源问题。提出双层次低秩近似A-最优设计目标，将问题解耦为离线阶段（预计算）和在线阶段（高效评估），实现无需额外PDE求解即可评估设计目标及其梯度。

Result: 所提算法能够高效扩展至大规模问题，得到的最优设计在最小化后验不确定性方面显著优于随机传感器布置。

Conclusion: 双层次低秩近似有效克服了被动成像OED的计算瓶颈，为大规模传感器布局优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This work focuses on optimal experimental design (OED) methods for passive imaging. We adopt a Bayesian inverse problem framework for passive imaging source problems, primarily focusing on spatially uncorrelated sources and systems governed by the Helmholtz equation. A major challenge in passive imaging is that the use of correlation data causes the observation dimension to grow quadratically with the number of sensor locations, compounding the computational difficulty of finding optimal designs. To overcome the computational bottleneck of repeated PDE solves in optimal design algorithms, we develop a two-level, low-rank approximation of the A-optimal design objective. This effectively decouples the problem into an offline and an online phase, enabling efficient evaluation of the design objective and its gradient without additional PDE solves. Our numerical results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm efficiently scales to large problems and that the resulting optimal designs significantly outperform random sensor placements in minimizing posterior uncertainty.

</details>


### [233] [On Parallel and Batch-Cutting Strategies for Norm-Minimization-Based Convex Vector Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05617)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对凸向量优化的基于范数最小化的外近似算法，提出了并行化（多工作线程同时求解子问题）和批量切割（每轮迭代添加最多K个支撑半空间）两种改进策略。批量切割变体继承了原始算法的收敛速度，实验表明8核并行加速1.1-4.2倍，批量切割减少62-80%的迭代次数，但加速效果依赖于顶点子问题计算成本是否占主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准凸向量优化外近似算法每轮迭代需求解N_k个独立子问题，但仅使用其中最优的一个切割，计算资源浪费严重。作者旨在通过并行化降低单轮迭代耗时，并通过批量切割充分利用所有已解子问题信息来减少总迭代次数。

Method: 提出两种改进：(1) 并行化：将每轮迭代中的子问题评估分布到nw个工作线程上，降低实际运行时间；(2) 批量切割：每轮迭代利用所有已解子问题的信息，添加最多K个支撑半空间（而非仅一个最优切割）。理论上证明了批量切割变体与原算法具有相同的收敛速率O(k^{2/(1-q)})。

Result: 在8个测试问题（q∈{2,3,4,5}）上，8核并行加速比在1.1到4.2之间；批量切割一致地减少了62-80%的迭代次数。但批量切割的实际耗时收益依赖于问题特性：由于每次迭代添加更多切割会加速顶点数量增长，因此当每个顶点的子问题计算成本占主导时，批量切割效果最佳。

Conclusion: 并行化和批量切割均可有效改进凸向量优化外近似算法，前者降低单轮时间，后者减少总迭代轮次。两种策略可结合使用，但批量切割的实际加速效果具有问题依赖性，需根据子问题计算成本与顶点数量增长之间的权衡来评估。

Abstract: We develop parallel and batch-cutting variants of the norm-minimization-based outer approximation algorithm for convex vector optimization. The standard algorithm solves $N_k$ independent subproblems at each iteration~$k$ to evaluate all vertices of the current polyhedral approximation, but processes only the single best cut. We propose two improvements. First, we parallelize the \revise{subproblem evaluations} across $\nw$ workers, reducing per-iteration wall-clock time. Second, we introduce a batch-cutting strategy that adds up to $K$ supporting halfspaces per iteration, using information from all solved subproblems rather than discarding it. We prove that the batch-cutting variant inherits the convergence rate $O(k^{2/(1-q)})$ of the standard algorithm, where $k$ is the number of outer iterations and $q$ is the number of objectives. Computational experiments on eight test problems with $q \in \{2,3,4,5\}$ show that parallelism on 8 cores \revise{increases the speed by a factor of 1.1 to 4.2}, and batch cutting consistently reduces the iteration count by 62--80\%. However, the wall-clock benefit of batch cutting is problem-dependent: the additional cuts per iteration accelerate vertex count growth, so batch cutting is most effective when per-vertex subproblem cost dominates.

</details>


### [234] [Convergence rate of the Halpern iterations with possibly distinct anchor and initial guess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05630)
*Jianing He,Qiao-Li Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了锚点与初始猜测可能不同的广义Halpern迭代的收敛速率，给出了预定和自适应锚定参数下的紧致收敛速率估计，推广了现有相关工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Halpern迭代的收敛速率估计均局限于锚点与初始猜测相同的情形，本文旨在填补锚点与初始猜测可能不同这一更一般情形下的理论空白。

Method: 针对预定锚定参数和自适应锚定参数两种策略，分别推导了广义Halpern迭代的紧致收敛速率估计。

Result: 获得了预定和自适应锚定参数下广义Halpern迭代的严格收敛速率上界，且这些估计是紧致的（即不可改进）。

Conclusion: 本文结果将现有Halpern迭代的收敛速率理论推广到了更一般的设定（锚点与初始猜测可不同），为后续算法设计与分析提供了更通用的理论基础。

Abstract: All existing convergence rate estimates of Halpern iterations are established only for the case where the anchor coincides with the initial guess. This paper aims to investigate the convergence rate of general Halpern iterations, where the anchor and the initial guess may not necessarily be the same. We present tight convergence rate estimates for both predetermined and adaptive anchoring parameters. These results generalize existing related work.

</details>


### [235] [Sparse Recovery via $\ell_1^2-η\ell_2^2$ Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05674)
*Lang Yu,Nan-jing Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对加权范数平方差（WDSN）惩罚项 $\ell_1^2-\eta\ell_2^2$（$0\leq\eta\leq1$）在压缩感知中的精确恢复保证和RIP分析空白，建立了基于零空间性质（NSP）的充分条件，推导了基于$\delta_{2k}$-RIP的稳定恢复误差上界，提出了统一的WDSN正则化模型，导出了近端算子的显式公式，设计了基于ADMM的求解算法并证明了全局收敛性，数值实验表明该方法在稀疏恢复任务中优于迭代半变分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WDSN惩罚项在压缩感知和逆问题中展现出强大的稀疏促进能力和优异的重构性能，但尚无精确恢复保证和RIP分析的理论结果，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 首先基于零空间性质（NSP）建立k-稀疏信号精确恢复的充分条件；其次在$\delta_{2k}$-RIP条件下推导稀疏信号和一般信号的稳定恢复误差上界；然后提出WDSN正则化统一框架处理无噪和噪声观测；推导WDSN泛函近端算子的显式公式，基于此设计变量分裂ADMM算法并给出全局收敛性证明。

Result: 建立了WDSN最小化的精确恢复理论保证和RIP分析结果；提出了高效的ADMM求解算法；数值实验表明该方法在无噪和有噪稀疏恢复任务中均优于迭代半变分方法。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地建立了WDSN惩罚项的理论恢复保证，并提供了高效算法，实验验证了其优越性能，为WDSN在稀疏恢复中的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: The weighted difference of squared norms (WDSN) penalty $\ell_1^2-η\ell_2^2$ with $0\leq η\leq 1$ has attracted considerable attention due to its strong sparsity-promoting ability and favorable reconstruction performance in compressed sensing and inverse problems. However, exact recovery guarantees and restricted isometry property (RIP) analysis for WDSN minimization have not yet been established. In this paper, we address this gap. First, we establish sufficient conditions for the exact recovery of $k$-sparse signals based on the null space property (NSP). Then, under the $δ_{2k}$-RIP condition, we derive stable recovery guarantees for both $k$-sparse signals and general signals, and characterize upper bounds on the reconstruction error. Furthermore, we propose a WDSN-based regularized model to handle both noiseless and noisy observations in a unified framework. To design an efficient algorithm, we derive an explicit formula for the proximal operator of the WDSN functional. Based on this proximal solver, we develop a suitable variable-splitting scheme within the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) and establish its global convergence under some mild conditions. Finally, numerical experiments show that the proposed method outperforms the iterative half variation method in both noiseless and noisy sparse recovery tasks.

</details>


### [236] [Combining diffuse and sharp interface methods in shape optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05963)
*Philip J. Herbert,Michael Hinze,Christian Kahle*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合相场法和尖锐界面法的两阶段形状优化策略。先用相场法确定形状、大小和拓扑，再以尖锐界面法在Lipschitz拓扑下进行精确更新，从而弥补相场法在锐化边界（如角点）方面的不足。两种方法均采用有限元离散，并通过相场解的零水平集构造尖锐法的初始网格。在多个测试问题中验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场法能有效处理形状、大小和拓扑优化，但在锐化边界（如生成角点）方面存在困难；而尖锐界面法擅长在Lipschitz拓扑下提供精确的形状更新。因此，将两者结合以取长补短。

Method: 两阶段过程：第一阶段用相场法确定优化形状；第二阶段以相场解作为尖锐界面法的初始解。两种方法均用有限元离散。通过后处理相场解的零水平集，从相场有限元网格构建尖锐法的初始网格。

Result: 在多个来自文献和应用的测试问题上验证了所提方法的性能。

Conclusion: 相场法与尖锐界面法的结合能够有效克服各自缺点，实现更精确的形状优化，尤其在需要锐化边界的情形下表现出优势。

Abstract: We develop a concept for the numerical treatment of shape optimization problems based on the combination of phase field and sharp interface methods. On the one hand, phase field methods are very well suited to numerically determine the shape, size and topology of a sought domain, but on the other hand they have problems to sharpen out domains where they e.g. should develop corners. However, this is the strength of a sharp-interface approach developed in our group, which provides shape updates in the Lipschitz topology. This leads to a two-stage process that first determines an optimized shape using the phase field method. The resulting domain is the starting solution for the sharp interface shape optimization method. Both methods are discretized with the finite element method. The starting mesh for the sharp method is constructed from the finite element mesh of the optimal phase field solution using its properly post processed zero-level set. We describe this construction process in detail and investigate the performance of our method on a selection of test problems from the literature and from applications.

</details>


### [237] [Distributionally robust shortfall risk portfolio model with moment ambiguity sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05996)
*Yi Yang,Liu Yang,Suhan Zhong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于矩的分布鲁棒优化（DRO）短差风险投资组合模型，利用多项式关系刻画股票收益与影响因素，将模型转化为含非负多项式锥的线性锥优化问题，并设计了Moment-SOS松弛算法，证明了算法的有限与渐近收敛性及全局最优性验证条件，实证表明方法有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统投资组合模型对收益分布假设过于严格，实际市场中收益与影响因素存在非线性关系，且分布不确定性会导致模型表现不佳。本文旨在利用短差风险度量并结合分布鲁棒优化，以处理参数不确定性和非线性依赖。

Method: 1) 假设股票收益与影响因素服从多项式关系；2) 构建基于矩信息的DRO短差风险模型；3) 对分段线性损失函数，将模型转化为含非负多项式锥的线性锥优化问题；4) 提出Moment-SOS松弛算法求解转化后的问题；5) 研究算法的有限与渐近收敛性；6) 给出验证全局最优性的便利条件。

Result: 算法具有有限与渐近收敛性，能够为原DRO问题提供可验证全局最优性的解，真实股票市场数据实验证实了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于矩的DRO短差风险模型能够有效处理收益与影响因素的非线性关系及分布不确定性，所提算法在理论上可收敛至全局最优，在实际市场中表现良好。

Abstract: This paper employs shortfall risk to measure portfolio risk. Assume stock returns follow polynomial relations with their influence factors. We propose a moment-based distributionally robust optimization (DRO) shortfall risk portfolio model. For piecewise linear loss functions, we show that this DRO model can be transformed into a tractable linear conic optimization problem with nonnegative polynomial cones. A Moment-SOS relaxation algorithm is proposed to solve the transformed problem. Its finite and asymptotic convergence properties are studied. For optimizers computed from our algorithm, we give convenient conditions verify their global optimality for the original DRO problem. Numerical experiments involving real stock market data are given to show the efficiency of our approach.

</details>


### [238] [Uniqueness and stability of Lagrange multipliers and associated qualification conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06067)
*Patrick Mehlitz,Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究抽象空间中约束优化问题拉格朗日乘子的唯一性与稳定性，深入分析了拉格朗日乘子映射的孤立平静性（isolated calmness），证明了该条件足以保证Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz约束规范，并在额外假设下与严格Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz条件等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束优化问题中拉格朗日乘子的唯一性和稳定性对于理论分析和算法设计至关重要。已有研究证明严格Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz条件蕴含拉格朗日乘子映射的孤立平静性，进而保证乘子唯一性。本文旨在深入探究孤立平静性这一性质本身，明确其与经典约束规范及严格条件之间的逻辑关系。

Method: 采用泛函分析和集值映射理论中的变分分析方法，系统研究拉格朗日乘子映射的孤立平静性。通过理论推导建立该条件与Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz约束规范及严格Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz条件之间的蕴含关系与等价性，并构造反例说明各假设的必要性。

Result: 证明了孤立平静性足以推出Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz约束规范；在额外假设下，孤立平静性与严格Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz条件等价；通过反例验证了所提假设的不可或缺性。

Conclusion: 孤立平静性是连接拉格朗日乘子唯一性与经典约束规范的关键桥梁，深化了对抽象空间约束优化问题中乘子稳定性理论的理解。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with uniqueness and stability of Lagrange multipliers for constrained optimization problems in abstract spaces. It is well known that validity of the strict Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz condition implies the so-called isolated calmness, a one-sided Lipschitz property tailored for set-valued mappings, of some Lagrange multiplier mapping associated with a perturbed version of the original optimization problem, and the latter indeed is enough to guarantee uniqueness of the Lagrange multiplier. The paper studies the isolated calmness of the Lagrange multiplier mapping in detail. Exemplary, it is shown that this condition is sufficient for the Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz constraint qualification and, in the presence of additional assumptions, even equivalent to the strict Robinson-Zowe-Kurcyusz condition. Illustrative examples are presented to underline the necessity of postulated assumptions.

</details>


### [239] [Second order splitting dynamics for stochastic monotone inclusions with closed loop distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06280)
*Wutao Si,Hamza Ennaji,Jalal Fadili*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了基于分布评估的前向-后向分裂算子的连续时间二阶动力学系统，用于求解Hilbert空间中极大单调算子与余强制算子和的零点问题，该框架自然适用于涉及决策依赖分布的随机优化（即表演性预测）。在一致单调性假设下，采用消失粘性阻尼系数证明了轨迹强收敛性及速度的快速渐近收敛率；当正则化算子强单调时，采用常数Polyak型阻尼系数建立了全局指数收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解极大单调算子与余强制算子之和的零点问题，该形式能够自然刻画决策依赖分布下的随机优化问题（表演性预测），为这类问题提供理论保证的求解方法。

Method: 提出连续时间二阶动力学系统，由分布评估的前向-后向分裂算子驱动；采用两种阻尼策略：消失粘性阻尼系数（用于一致单调性假设）和常数Polyak型阻尼系数（用于强单调性假设）。

Result: 在一致单调性假设下，轨迹强收敛到平衡点，速度具有快速渐近收敛率；在强单调性假设下，系统具有全局指数收敛率。

Conclusion: 所提出的二阶动力学系统为表演性预测等决策依赖分布优化问题提供了理论上的收敛保证，不同阻尼策略适用于不同单调性条件下的优化求解。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the problem of finding a zero of the sum of a maximal monotone operator $A$ and a cocoercive operator $\Bm$ in a Hilbert space. This formulation naturally captures stochastic optimization problems with decision-dependent distributions, often referred to as performative prediction. We propose and analyze continuous-time second-order dynamics governed by a distributionally evaluated forward-backward splitting operator. We establish the existence and uniqueness of the equilibrium point under a general uniform monotonicity assumption. In this setting, employing a vanishing viscous damping coefficient, we prove the strong convergence of the trajectories to the equilibrium, accompanied by fast asymptotic convergence rates for the velocities. Furthermore, when the regularizing operator is strongly monotone, we consider a constant Polyak-type damping coefficient and we establish global exponential convergence rates for the dynamical system.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [How Far Did They Go? The Persuasive Tactics of Covert LLM Agents in a Discontinued Field Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05256)
*Kokil Jaidka,Saifuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了Reddit r/ChangeMyView论坛中一个已被叫停的AI实验数据。该实验由外部研究人员未经披露地使用AI生成账号与用户进行实时辩论，因伦理争议被终止。研究对AI评论进行了结构化内容分析，发现超三分之二的评论涉及身份定位，几乎所有评论都包含结盟策略和权威主张，绝大多数触发了认知偏见（尤其是确认偏误、代表性启发和可得性启发）。这些模式系统性地共现，构建了旨在说服效率而非真实参与的修辞架构。与人类反驳相比，AI在每一维度均呈反向分布：更密集的权威使用、更具对抗性的结盟、更依赖外部引用而非经验论证。研究指出，AI与人类认知地位的区分日益模糊，仅靠披露要求无法解决，需要建立评估AI如何构建可信性的审计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该数据集提供了罕见的自然主义场景，可观察大语言模型在未被披露的情况下如何在具有丰富身份表达的论坛中运作，从而研究AI在真实社交互动中的修辞策略和影响机制。

Method: 对Reddit r/ChangeMyView论坛中AI生成评论语料库进行结构化内容分析，评估身份表现、权威信号、结盟策略和认知启发激活模式，并与人类撰写的反驳论点进行比较。

Result: AI评论系统地运用身份定位（超三分之二）、结盟策略和权威主张（几乎所有评论）以及认知偏见触发（绝大多数）。这些模式共现形成以说服效率为导向的修辞架构。与人类相比，AI在权威使用密度、对抗性结盟和外部引用依赖上均显著更高。

Conclusion: 在AI嵌入的讨论环境中，真实与合成认知地位之间的界限日益模糊，仅靠披露无法解决问题。需要建立能够评估AI如何构建可信性的审计框架，而不仅仅是检测AI是否存在。

Abstract: This study analyzes a publicly released dataset from a discontinued field experiment on Reddit's r/ChangeMyView. The intervention, conducted by unknown, external researchers and halted following ethical backlash, involved undisclosed AI-generated accounts engaging users in live debate. After public disclosure, Reddit authorized moderators to release an archive of the AI-generated comments, creating a rare opportunity to examine how large language models operated in an identity-rich deliberative forum without disclosure. We conduct a structured content analysis of this corpus, evaluating identity performance, authority signaling, alignment strategies, and activation of cognitive heuristics. Identity targeting or adoption appears in over two-thirds of comments, alignment moves and authority claims in nearly all of them, and cognitive-bias triggers -- particularly confirmation bias, representativeness, and availability -- in the large majority. These patterns co-occur systematically, composing a rhetorical architecture calibrated for persuasive efficiency rather than authentic deliberative participation. Compared against human-authored CMV counter-arguments, the agents inverted the typical distribution on every dimension: denser authority use, more adversarial alignment, and heavier reliance on external citation over experiential grounding. In such environments, distinctions between authentic and synthetic epistemic standing grow increasingly opaque -- an asymmetry that disclosure mandates alone cannot address. The results point toward auditing frameworks capable of assessing how AI systems structure credibility, not merely whether they are present.

</details>


### [241] [What Should Agents Say? Action-state Communication for Efficient Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05304)
*Chen Huang,Yuhao Wu,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACT（协议化动作-状态通信与传输）方法，将多智能体系统（MAS）中智能体间的自由文本通信转化为紧凑的动作-状态记录，从而显著减少token消耗，提升性能-成本权衡。实验证明PACT在不同MAS拓扑下均能保持或提升任务性能的同时大幅降低token使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中，智能体间的通信采用无约束的自然语言，导致token使用量快速膨胀、共享上下文窗口被迅速消耗，进而影响系统性能和推理成本。

Method: 分析了五种常见的智能体间通信策略在两种MAS拓扑下的表现，发现没有固定策略普遍最优；提出PACT方法，将智能体间通信视为公开状态更新问题，在每个原始智能体输出进入共享历史之前，将其投影为紧凑的动作-状态记录。

Result: PACT在不同MAS拓扑下一致地改善了性能-成本权衡。在OpenHands中，以-10% tokens-per-resolved的代价提升了解析率；在SWE-agent上保持解析率中性同时将输入token减半。

Conclusion: PACT是一种有效的MAS通信优化方法，通过协议化动作-状态通信实现了性能和成本的更好平衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models are typically organized around roles, pipelines, and turn schedules, while the content that agents pass to one another is often left as unconstrained natural language. However, this free-form communication can rapidly inflate token usage, consume the shared context window, and ultimately affect both system performance and inference cost. We analyze five common inter-agent communication strategies across two MAS topologies, finding that no fixed strategy is universally optimal. Instead, effective inter-agent messages consistently preserve action-centered information needed by downstream agents. Building on this, we propose the PACT (Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission), which treats inter-agent communication as a public state-update problem and projects each raw agent output into a compact action-state record before it enters shared history. Across different MAS topologies, PACT consistently improves the performance-cost trade-off, achieving comparable or stronger task performance with substantially fewer tokens. The gains extend to production coding harnesses: PACT lifts OpenHands' resolve rate at -10% tokens-per-resolved, and is resolve-neutral on SWE-agent while halving input tokens. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/PACT.

</details>


### [242] [I Know What You Meme, Even If it Emerged Today: Understanding Evolving Memes through Open-World Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05316)
*Shanhong Liu,Rui Cao,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Query Retrieve Conclude (QRC)零样本框架，通过识别缺失知识、检索开放网络证据、合成证据支撑的背景知识来理解多模态模因，在2024-2026年新模因基准测试中优于零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模因具有动态性，需最新背景知识理解，现有方法依赖预训练模型的固定参数知识，这些知识可能不完整、过时或无法获取新兴模因相关信息。

Method: 提出Query Retrieve Conclude (QRC)零样本框架：1) 识别缺失知识；2) 检索开放网络证据；3) 合成基于证据的背景知识，用于模因理解和检测。

Result: 在3个模因理解数据集和5个模因检测任务上，QRC框架在知识恢复、模因理解及下游检测任务中均优于零样本基线。

Conclusion: 通过引入外部开放网络知识检索与合成方法，可有效弥补预训练模型对新兴模因的知识缺口，提升模因理解与检测性能。

Abstract: Multimodal memes are dynamic and often require up to date background knowledge for interpretation. Existing methods often overlook such knowledge or rely on fixed parametric knowledge of pretrained models that may be incomplete, outdated, or unavailable for emerging memes. We introduce Query Retrieve Conclude, a zero shot framework that identifies missing knowledge, retrieves open web evidence, and synthesizes evidence grounded background knowledge for meme understanding and detection. We also introduce a curated meme understanding benchmark of recent memes from 2024 to 2026 with external background knowledge annotations. Experiments on three meme understanding datasets and five meme detection tasks show that our framework improves knowledge recovery, meme understanding and downstream detection over zero shot baselines.

</details>


### [243] [GITCO: Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization in TSFMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05332)
*Manya Pandey,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出GITCO框架，通过推理时优化输入上下文（而非修改模型权重），选择性地识别和抑制有害的异常块，从而提升基于块的时序基础模型（TSFMs）的零样本预测质量。在TimesFM 2.5上评估，平均MASE降低1.95%，达到改进上界的89.9%。同时提出上下文敏感度配置文件作为TSFMs的新可表征属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于块的时序基础模型（TSFMs）存在上下文中毒问题：结构异常的块会不成比例地吸引模型注意力，并在零样本预测中无声地降低预测质量。

Method: 提出GITCO（门控推理时上下文优化）框架，包含三个轻量级组件：Gate（门控）、Router（路由器）和Critic（评论家），在不更新任何参数的情况下，选择性识别并抑制有害的输入块。

Result: 在53个GIFT-Eval数据集上对TimesFM 2.5进行K折交叉验证，GITCO平均降低MASE 1.95%，达到改进上界的89.9%。

Conclusion: 提出上下文敏感度配置文件（context sensitivity profiles）作为TSFMs的新可表征属性，该属性描述时序元特征与推理时上下文干预下预期精度改进之间的映射关系，由模型架构和数据统计结构共同塑造。

Abstract: Patch-based Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) suffer from context poisoning: structurally anomalous patches capture disproportionate attention and silently degrade zero-shot forecast quality. We propose improving TSFM accuracy at inference time by optimizing the input context rather than modifying model weights. We present GITCO (Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization), a lightweight three-component framework: Gate, Router, and Critic that selectively identifies and suppresses harmful patches without any parameter updates. Evaluated on TimesFM 2.5 across 53 GIFT-Eval datasets under K-fold cross-validation, GITCO achieves an average +1.95% MASE reduction on TimesFM 2.5 while capturing 89.9% of the improvement upper bound. We introduce context sensitivity profiles as a new characterizable property of TSFMs: the mapping from time series meta-features to expected accuracy improvement under inference-time context intervention, shaped jointly by model architecture and the statistical structure of the data.

</details>


### [244] [Uncertainty Aware Functional Behavior Prediction and Material Fatigue Assessment for Circular Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05334)
*Nehal Afifi,Mehdi Khabou,Victor Mas,Jonas Hemmerich,Patric Grauberger,Stefan Dietrich,Volker Schulze,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对角磨机提出了一种结合不确定性感知功能预测与组件级疲劳评估的实例特定可靠性工作流。通过卷积编码器提取载荷模式、LSTM预测九个功能变量的高斯均值和方差，并同步进行有限元支撑的疲劳损伤与裂纹扩展分析。测试显示平均2%容差精度为0.9652，其中热变量预测近乎完美，驱动电机电流和负载速度最具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回收工厂中的返回产品具有异质性退化状态、使用历史和剩余能力。仅靠当前检查无法决定重用，因为未来功能实现和组件完整性在下一次服务场景中可能不同演化。现有PHM方法通常针对固定操作条件或孤立组件基准，材料疲劳评估很少与系统级功能预后相关联。

Method: 提出结合不确定性感知功能预测与组件级疲劳评估的框架。卷积编码器提取主轴力和轴扭矩中的载荷模式，LSTM骨干网络预测九个功能变量作为高斯均值和方差估计。同时，相同载荷历史通过有限元支持的应力重建、S-N/Miner损伤评估（含Haibach扩展）和Paris定律裂纹增长分析转化为输出轴疲劳信息。流式回放算法将两分支整合为功能、材料和系统可靠性轨迹。

Result: 留出测试显示九个输出变量的平均2%容差精度为0.9652。热变量预测近乎完美，驱动电机电流（R²=0.9750）和负载速度（R²=0.9924）是最具挑战性的动态输出。扭矩历史对这些变量尤为重要，传统LSTM在短历史设置中优于GRU和xLSTM。驱动电机电流的可靠性校准信息量最大。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功弥合了组件级疲劳评估与系统级功能预后之间的差距，为循环工厂中异质性返回产品的重用决策提供了实例特定的可靠性评估方法。

Abstract: Returned products in circular factories re-enter production with heterogeneous degradation states, usage histories, and remaining capability. Reuse cannot be decided from the current inspection alone, because future function fulfillment and component integrity may evolve differently under the next service scenario. Existing PHM approaches support degradation prediction, but often target fixed operating conditions or isolated component benchmarks, while material-fatigue assessment is rarely linked to system-level functional prognosis. This paper addresses this gap for an angle grinder by combining uncertainty-aware functional prediction with component-level fatigue assessment in an instance-specific reliability workflow. The proposed framework combines the current tool state with recent force--torque usage windows. A convolutional encoder extracts loading patterns from spindle forces and shaft torque, and an LSTM backbone predicts nine functional variables as Gaussian mean and variance estimates. In parallel, the same loading history is translated into output-shaft fatigue information through finite-element-supported stress reconstruction, S--N/Miner damage evaluation with Haibach extension, and Paris-law crack-growth analysis. A streaming replay algorithm consolidates both branches into functional, material, and system reliability trajectories. Held-out tests show mean \(2\%\)-tolerance accuracy of 0.9652 across nine outputs. Thermal variables are predicted near-perfectly, while drive motor current and load speed remain the most demanding dynamic outputs, with \(R^2\) values of 0.9750 and 0.9924. Torque history is especially important for these variables, and the conventional LSTM outperforms GRU and xLSTM in the short-history setting. Reliability calibration is most informative for drive motor current, where predicted and observed exceedance probabilities ...

</details>


### [245] [SentinelBench: A Benchmark for Long-Running Monitoring Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05342)
*Matheus Kunzler Maldaner,Adam Fourney,Amanda Swearngin,Hussein Mozzanar,Gagan Bansal,Maya Murad,Rafah Hosn,Saleema Amershi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SentinelBench基准，用于评估AI代理在长时间跨度任务中采用"持续关注"而非"持续行动"策略的能力，该基准包含100个任务、10个合成网络环境，衡量任务完成率、响应时间和资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理默认采用持续行动模式（不断调用工具、刷新页面等），这对于需要长时间等待外部事件的任务效率低下且浪费资源。代理应具备"持续关注"能力：监测环境、等待外部事件、及时响应而不浪费资源。

Method: 构建SentinelBench基准，包含100个任务分布在10个合成网络环境中（邮件、日历、金融、专业社交、娱乐等）。每个环境提供实时网络界面并回放预编的事件序列，要求代理在状态变化的网页上进行导航和推理。测量指标包括任务完成率、响应时间和资源使用量。

Result: 在3种模型和2种浏览器代理框架上建立性能基线，结果表明SentinelBench能够区分代理行为中的显著差异，且代理设计选择对关键指标有显著影响。

Conclusion: 对于长时间跨度任务，代理应采取"持续关注"策略而非"持续行动"策略；SentinelBench能有效衡量代理在这类任务中的表现，揭示响应性与成本之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly asked to carry out work that spans minutes, hours, or longer. Yet the default model of agent behavior is continuous action: issuing tool calls, refreshing pages, searching for alternatives, or otherwise trying to force progress. This is the wrong approach for many long-running tasks, which are better served by a strategy of sustained attention. Instead, agents should monitor an environment, notice when an external event makes progress possible, then respond promptly without wasting resources while waiting. To measure progress on this class of tasks, we introduce SentinelBench, an open-source benchmark for time-evolving monitoring tasks.
  SentinelBench contains 100 tasks across 10 synthetic web environments, including email, calendars, finance, professional networking, and entertainment. Each environment exposes a live web interface and replays a scripted sequence of events, requiring agents to navigate and reason about web pages whose state shifts underfoot. SentinelBench measures task completion, reaction time, and resource use, exposing the tradeoff between responsiveness and cost. We report results across three models and two browser-agent harnesses, establishing performance baselines for future comparison and demonstrating how agent design choices can dramatically impact key metrics. Together, these results show that SentinelBench distinguishes meaningful differences in agent behavior.

</details>


### [246] [An interpretable and trustworthy AI framework for large-scale longitudinal structure-pain association studies using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05357)
*Jincheng Yu,Haoyang Li,Yiwen Liu,Shen Liu,Rachel Yuanbao Chen,C. Kent Kwoh,Hongxu Ding,Xiaoxiao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释且可信的AI框架，结合深度学习进行MRI膝关节骨关节炎评分（MOAKS）预测与可解释统计建模，用于大规模研究膝关节结构与疼痛之间的关系。通过共形预测提供不确定性量化，筛选高置信度预测，并利用纵向潜在类别混合模型（LCMM）分析结构异常与疼痛的关系。结果显示，骨 marrow 病变（BML）、软骨丢失（CART）和半月板挤压（ME）的预测性能显著提升，且这些结构异常是疼痛快速进展的重要风险因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在医学影像分析中缺乏可解释性和不确定性量化，难以可靠地用于大规模结构-疼痛关系研究。本文旨在开发一个既准确又可信的AI框架，以克服这些局限。

Method: 1) 深度学习框架预测MOAKS特征，结合共形预测进行不确定性量化，仅保留高置信度预测。2) 纵向潜在类别混合模型（LCMM）分析BML、CART和ME三种结构异常与四种互补性膝关节疼痛测量之间的关联。

Result: BML的MCC从0.69提升至0.91，CART从0.45提升至0.80，ME从0.59提升至0.89。样本量扩展至2,175个膝关节。识别出快速和稳定两种疼痛进展轨迹。快速进展组中，BML的OR=1.62(1.12-2.35)，CART的OR=1.83(1.24-2.70)，ME的OR=2.50(1.75-3.57)。

Conclusion: BML、CART和ME是骨关节炎疼痛和功能进展的重要风险因素，该框架为大规模结构-疼痛关系研究提供了可靠的方法学支持。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop an interpretable and trustworthy AI framework that combines deep learning based MRI Osteoarthritis Knee Score (MOAKS) prediction with interpretable statistical modeling to study structure-pain relationships at scale using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI). Materials and Methods: We first developed a deep learning framework to predict MOAKS features directly from knee MRIs and incorporated conformal prediction to provide prediction uncertainty quantification. This uncertainty-aware strategy enables explicit filtering of model outputs, retaining only high-confidence MOAKS predictions at the knee level. Second, we applied a longitudinal latent class mixed model (LCMM) to examine associations between key structural abnormalities and four complementary knee pain measurements. Results: Among the three MRI-defined abnormalities (i.e., bone marrow lesions (BML), cartilage loss (CART), and meniscal extrusion (ME)), our framework substantially improved the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) and some other metrics. For example, MCC increased from 0.69 to 0.91 for BML, from 0.45 to 0.80 for CART, and from 0.59 to 0.89 for ME. Using these high-confidence predictions, we expanded the sample size to 2,175 knees for the LCMM analysis. Two distinct pain trajectories were identified (rapid and stable pain progression). The estimated odds ratios (95% CI) for the rapid progression group were 1.62 (1.12-2.35) for BML, 1.83 (1.24-2.70) for CART loss, and 2.50 (1.75-3.57) for ME. Conclusion: These results highlight the importance of these structural abnormalities as risk factors for pain and functional progression in osteoarthritis.

</details>


### [247] [Synthetic Contrastive Reasoning for Multi-Table Q&A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05382)
*Ankit Pratap Singh,Xin Su,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多表问答缺乏推理监督的问题，构建了合成对比推理轨迹数据集，并通过对比偏好优化（CPO）微调开源LLM，在MMQA上取得9.7%-16.3%的绝对平均提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多表问答资源仅提供问题和最终答案，缺乏解释答案推导过程的推理监督信号，限制了模型推理能力的提升。

Method: 利用异构LLM生成经过验证的正向推理轨迹和合理的负向推理轨迹，构建合成对比推理轨迹数据集；使用对比偏好优化（CPO）在偏好对上微调开源大语言模型。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B、Mistral-8B和Llama-3.1-8B上，CPO相较标准有监督微调实现9.7%-16.3%的绝对平均提升，在MMQA上最高提升21个百分点。

Conclusion: 异构正负轨迹生成器能增强对比信号，生成的偏好对具有高忠实度、连贯性和对比意义，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-table question answering requires models to retrieve relevant evidence, link schemas, and perform compositional reasoning across relational tables. Existing multi-table Q&A resources typically provide questions and final answers but lack reasoning supervision that explains how answers are derived. To address this gap, we construct a synthetic contrastive reasoning-trace dataset for MMQA by generating validated positive traces and plausible negative traces with heterogeneous LLMs. We then use the resulting preference pairs to fine-tune open-weight LLMs with Contrastive Preference Optimization (CPO). Across Qwen3-14B, Mistral-8B, and Llama-3.1-8B, CPO achieves absolute average improvements over Q&A supervised fine-tuning ranging from 9.7%-16.3%, with gains up to 21 percentage points on MMQA. Ablations show that heterogeneous positive and negative trace generators strengthen the contrastive signal, and automated as well as human evaluations indicate that the generated pairs are largely faithful, coherent, and meaningfully contrastive.

</details>


### [248] [Stability vs. Manipulability: Evaluating Robustness Under Post-Decision Interaction in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05384)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM评估者在静态场景下判断稳定，但在后续交互中容易被定向挑战逆转，且这种逆转会降低与人类偏好的一致性、改变基准排名，并伴随事后合理化解释。作者提出评估鲁棒性分数（ERS）来量化交互鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示LLM-as-judge评估在交互场景下存在的后决策可操纵性问题，即评估结果并非输入的稳定属性，而是可以通过后续对话被改变。

Method: 在MT-Bench和AlpacaEval上设计对照实验，采用抗基线挑战协议和平衡目标验证协议，系统性地测试LLM评估者在重复评估、中性重评和定向挑战下的稳定性，并引入ERS指标量化交互鲁棒性。

Result: LLM评估者在重复和中立重评下高度稳定，但在定向后决策挑战下显著可逆；权威框架会加剧不稳定性；修正后的判断常伴随低重叠的理由，表明是事后合理化而非可靠纠错；这些逆转会降低与人类偏好的一致性并改变基准排名。

Conclusion: 后决策交互是LLM-as-judge评估的一个独特失效模式，现有仅测量静态一致性的评估协议不足，应考虑在挑战下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge evaluation is widely used in benchmarking pipelines, where model outputs are compared and ranked using automated evaluators. These pipelines typically assume that judgments are stable properties of fixed inputs. We show that this assumption does not hold under interaction. We study post-decision manipulability: the extent to which an evaluation outcome can be altered through subsequent conversation with the judge after an initial decision has been made. Across controlled experiments on MT-Bench and AlpacaEval, we find that LLM judges are highly stable under repeated and neutral reevaluation, yet become substantially reversible under targeted post-decision challenge. An anti-baseline challenge protocol shows that stable judgments can be overturned through motivated interaction, while a counterbalanced target-validation protocol separates this reversibility from net target-directed steering. These reversals have practical consequences: they can degrade agreement with human preferences, shift benchmark rankings, and produce harmful evaluation changes despite high self-reported confidence. Authority framing is especially destabilizing, and revised judgments are often accompanied by low-overlap justifications, suggesting post hoc rationalization rather than reliable error correction. We introduce the Evaluation Robustness Score (ERS) to quantify interactional robustness by combining reversal susceptibility with counterbalanced directional effects. Our findings identify post-decision interaction as a distinct failure mode for LLM-as-judge evaluation and motivate evaluation protocols that measure not only static agreement, but robustness under challenge.

</details>


### [249] [Residual Modeling for High-Fidelity Learned Compression of Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05389)
*Liangji Zhu,Sanjay Ranka,Anand Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对有损压缩中保证性自编码器在高保真度下残差校正流占用码率过高的问题，提出残差导向视角，设计了两种残差编码器LBRC和NGLR，通过为学习残差定制编码表示，在10^-6~10^-4 NRMSE范围内相比GAE提升30-60%和10-40%的压缩比，优于SZ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保证性自编码器方法在高保真度场景下，由于保留的SVD/PCA系数快速增长，残差校正流占据主导码率，导致压缩效率显著下降。需要一种更适合学习残差特性的编码方法来保持学习压缩的优势。

Method: 提出残差导向视角，设计了两种残差编码器：LBRC（确定性免训练流水线，通过自适应量化和3D Lorenzo差分、Z字形映射、位平面编码及熵编码进行无损编码）和NGLR（引入因果神经预测器，为整数取整的Lorenzo预测提供归一化偏置，减少残差熵的同时保持确定性解码）。

Result: 在E3SM、JHTDB、ERA5数据集上，以10^-6到10^-4块级NRMSE为目标，LBRC相比GAE提升30-60%压缩比，与SZ相当；NGLR相比LBRC进一步提升10-40%，在该高保真度范围内优于SZ。

Conclusion: 针对学习压缩器残差定制的表示方法能够在全局残差校正成为码率主导因素时，保持学习压缩的优势。

Abstract: Lossy compression is essential for massive spatiotemporal data from scientific simulations. Learned compressors can achieve high compression ratios at moderate accuracy targets, but their aggregate reconstruction losses do not guarantee accuracy for each block. Existing Guaranteed Autoencoder (GAE) methods add a per-block residual correction by retaining SVD/PCA-style coefficients until the target is met. This works at moderate tolerances, but in the high-fidelity regime with block-level NRMSE from 10^-6 to 10^-4, the number of retained coefficients grows quickly and the correction stream dominates the total rate.
  We propose a residual-centric view: the learned residual is structurally different from the original scientific field and should be coded with a representation designed for that residual. We introduce two residual coders. LBRC is a deterministic, training-free pipeline that adaptively quantizes the learned residual to the target NRMSE and losslessly encodes the resulting integer residual using 3D Lorenzo differencing, zigzag mapping, bit-plane coding, and entropy coding. NGLR adds a causal neural predictor that outputs a normalized bias for an integer-rounded Lorenzo prediction in the same deterministic integer pipeline, reducing the entropy of the remaining residual code while preserving deterministic decoding. The predictor weights are serialized and counted in the bitstream.
  Across E3SM, JHTDB, and ERA5 at block-level NRMSE targets from 10^-6 to 10^-4, LBRC improves compression ratio over GAE by 30-60% and is broadly competitive with SZ. NGLR adds a further 10-40% over LBRC and outperforms SZ in the evaluated high-fidelity regime. These results show that residual representations tailored to learned-compressor residuals can preserve the advantage of learned compression when global residual correction becomes rate-dominant.

</details>


### [250] [LeanMarathon: Toward Reliable AI Co-Mathematicians through Long-Horizon Lean Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05400)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Yuekai Sun,Taiji Suzuki,Jason D. Lee,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LeanMarathon，一个用于研究级数学自动形式化的多智能体框架。其核心是演化蓝图——同时作为形式化证明骨架、自然语言证明图和共享系统记录。通过两阶段协调器（先对抗性审查稳定目标保真度，再从动态叶节点向上并行证明DAG），在Lean中成功形式化了四个Erdős问题的七个目标定理，证明258个引理和定理，无sorry。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程研究数学的自动形式化不仅在困难引理上失败，在规模化时也面临陈述漂移、依赖缠绕、上下文衰减和局部修复破坏远程工作等问题。需要一种能够保持目标保真度的可靠框架。

Method: 提出LeanMarathon，核心是演化蓝图（Lean文件同时作为形式化证明骨架、NL证明图和系统记录）。四个合约范围智能体（构建、审计、证明、修复）由两阶段协调器协调：第一阶段通过对抗性审查稳定目标保真度，第二阶段从动态叶节点向上并行CI门控轮次证明DAG。

Result: 在涵盖四个Erdős问题（#1051, #1196, #164, #1217）的两篇近期研究论文上评估，三次自主运行形式化所有七个目标定理，证明258个引理和定理，无sorry。

Conclusion: 可靠的AI协同数学不仅需要更强的证明器，还需要能够在长程数学开发中保持目标保真度的持久化框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon autoformalization of research mathematics fails not only at hard lemmas, but at scale: statements drift, dependencies tangle, context decays, and local repairs corrupt distant work. We present LeanMarathon, a multi-agent harness for reliable research-level Lean autoformalization. Its core abstraction is an evolving blueprint: a Lean file that serves simultaneously as formal proof skeleton, natural-language proof graph, and shared system of record. Four contract-scoped agents construct, audit, prove, and repair this blueprint. These agents are coordinated by a two-stage orchestrator that first stabilizes target fidelity through adversarial review and then discharges the proof directed acyclic graph (DAG) from its dynamic leaves upward in parallel CI-gated rounds. LeanMarathon turns one brittle multi-hour run into many local, recoverable, parallel transactions. We evaluate LeanMarathon on two recent research papers spanning four Erdős problems (#1051, #1196, #164, #1217). Across three autonomous runs, it formalizes all seven target theorems with no sorry, proving 258 lemmas and theorems. These results show that reliable AI co-mathematics requires not only stronger provers, but durable harnesses that preserve target fidelity across long mathematical developments. The code can be found at https://github.com/YuanheZ/LeanMarathon.

</details>


### [251] [Harnessing Generalist Agents for Contextualized Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05404)
*Zihao Li,Kaifeng Jin,Yuanchen Bei,Jiaru Zou,Avaneesh Kumar,Xuying Ning,Yanjun Zhao,Mengting Ai,Baoyu Jing,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeClaw是一个针对时间序列的智能体框架，为通用LLM代理提供时间序列原生运行时支持，通过可执行的时间工具、经验驱动的能力进化和情景多模态记忆，实现上下文感知的时间推理，在能源、金融、天气、交通等多个领域的基准测试中展现了更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列常嵌入丰富的上下文中，而实际工作流中如预测等任务只是更广泛解决方案的一环。通用AI代理虽提供了有前景的接口，但主要运行在文本空间中，与结构化时间信号未完全对齐。

Method: TimeClaw包含三个核心组件：可执行的时间工具（用于基于证据的可审计分析）、经验驱动的能力进化（创建可复用的分析例程）、以及情景多模态记忆（检索相关推理轨迹）。

Result: 在能源、金融、天气、交通等多个真实世界领域的多样化任务基准测试中，TimeClaw展现了更优的性能。

Conclusion: TimeClaw通过为通用LLM代理提供时间序列原生运行时支持，有效解锁了带有上下文信息的开放式时间推理能力，提升了时间序列分析的性能。

Abstract: Time series are often embedded in rich contexts that are essential for holistic modeling. Moreover, real-world practitioners often require end-to-end workflows for analyzing temporal dynamics, where widely studied tasks such as forecasting are only one step in a broader solution loop. While generalist AI agents offer a promising interface for such workflows under complex contexts, they still operate primarily in textual spaces that are not fully aligned with structured temporal signals. In this work, we introduce TimeClaw, an agentic harness framework for time series that equips generalist LLM agents with the time series-native runtime support needed for contextualized temporal reasoning. TimeClaw integrates executable temporal tools for grounded and auditable analysis, experience-driven capability evolution for creating reusable analytical routines, and episodic multimodal memory for retrieving relevant reasoning traces. Together, these components unlock harnessed open-ended temporal reasoning with contextual information. Extensive evaluation on multiple benchmarks covering diverse tasks across energy, finance, weather, traffic, and other real-world domains demonstrates improved performance of TimeClaw. Code is available at https://github.com/iDEA-iSAIL-Lab-UIUC/TimeClaw.

</details>


### [252] [Agents' Last Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05405)
*Yiyou Sun,Xinyang Han,Weichen Zhang,Yuanbo Pang,Tianyu Wang,Yuhan Cao,Yixiao Huang,Chris Duroiu,Haoyun Zhang,Jeffrey Lin,Weishu Zhang,Tyler Zeng,Ying Yan,Bo Liu,Hanson Wen,Mingyang Xu,Xiaoyuan Xu,Ruiling Xu,Mingxuan Yan,Benjamin Yang,Jirong Yang,Sen Yang,Xiaoli Yang,Yushi Yang,Haoran Ye,Xiaohu Yu,Zhengming Yu,Chenlong Zhang,Chi Zhang,Hanning Zhang,Hanwen Zhang,Junge Zhang,Kunpeng Zhang,Song Zhang,Wenjin Zhang,Wenshuo Zhang,Ying Zhang,Yizhi Zhang,Brian Zhao,Qijian Zhao,Yimin Zhao,Yuhaohua Zheng,Liwei Zhou,Tianyue Zhou,Sichen Zhu,Siqi Zhu,Yan Zhu,Yishu Zhu,Jierui Zuo,Chonghao Cai,Helena Shi,Haoran Wu,Zhiyue Wu,Hannah Yao,Zhuoran Yi,Jenny Yu,Rhea Zhan,Hang Zhou,Blake Zhu,Junfan Zhu,Alan Yuille,Yang Liu,Russell Alan Poldrack,Jiachen Li,Zhenglu Li,Molei Tao,Jing Huang,Wenqi Shi,Costas Spanos,Lichao Sun,Chenguang Wang,Orson Xu,Zhen Dong,Hector Gomez,Aylin Caliskan,Ali Emami,Haimin Hu,Zhi Li,Lihui Liu,Murphy Niu,Yi Shao,Jianxin Sun,Mikko Tolonen,Ting Wang,Sanjiv Das,Yanjun Gao,Wenbo Guo,Erika J Schneider,Zhiyong Lu,Mark Mueller,Radha Poovendran,Somayeh Sojoudi,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agents' Last Exam (ALE)基准，用于评估AI代理在长期、经济价值高的真实世界任务上的表现。该基准与250+行业专家合作开发，覆盖55个子领域、13个行业集群、1000+任务。当前结果显示最困难层级平均通过率仅2.6%，表明基准远未饱和。ALE设计为动态增长的基准，旨在缩小基准测试成功与GDP相关影响之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在众多基准测试上表现出色，但这些成果并未转化为许多专业领域中有经济意义的实际部署。作者认为这一差距主要源于评估问题：广泛使用的基准缺乏对真实且具有经济价值工作流的持续性绩效测量。

Method: 与250+行业专家合作，基于O*NET/SOC 2018美国联邦职业分类体系，构建了包含55个子领域、13个行业集群、1000+任务的基准。ALE专注于非体力行业，评估AI代理在长期、真实世界、可验证结果的经济价值任务上的表现。

Result: 在当前主流配置下，最困难层级的平均完全通过率仅为2.6%，表明该基准远未饱和。

Conclusion: ALE设计为动态增长的基准，任务池随新工作流和行业加入而持续扩展。其目的不仅是另一个排行榜，而是作为弥合基准测试成功与GDP相关影响之间差距的工具。

Abstract: Recent AI systems have achieved strong results on a wide range of benchmarks, yet these gains have not translated into economically meaningful deployment across many professional domains. We argue that this gap is largely an evaluation problem: widely used benchmarks lack sustained performance measurement on real and economically valuable workflows. This paper introduces Agents' Last Exam (ALE), a benchmark designed to evaluate AI agents on long-horizon, economically valuable, real-world tasks with verifiable outcomes. Developed in collaboration with 250+ industry experts, ALE covers non-physical industries defined with reference to O*NET / SOC 2018 (the U.S. federal occupational taxonomy). It is organized around a task taxonomy with 55 subfields grouped into 13 industry clusters covering 1K+ tasks. Current results show that the hardest tier remains far from saturated: across mainstream harness and backbone configurations, the average full pass rate is 2.6%. ALE is designed as a living benchmark: its task pool grows continuously as new workflows and industries are onboarded. More broadly, ALE is intended not merely as another leaderboard, but as an instrument for closing the gap between benchmark success and GDP-relevant impact.

</details>


### [253] [Mutation Without Variation: Convergence Dynamics in LLM-Driven Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05408)
*Can Gurkan,Forrest Stonedahl,Uri Wilensky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，LLM驱动的程序突变在没有选择压力的情况下，会持续收敛到受限的吸引子区域，87%的链中超过93%的突变重复了之前的结构形式，而经典GP子树突变算子不会出现这种收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在反复突变程序时，是否能够探索新的程序形式，还是会循环回到已有的形式。这关系到LLM驱动的程序进化系统能否维持开放式探索。

Method: 在领域特定语言中分析LLM驱动的突变链，排除选择压力，变化提示设计、模型家族和随机复制因素。通过结构级和词法级分析突变轨迹，并与经典GP子树突变算子对比。

Result: LLM突变持续收敛到程序空间中的受限吸引子区域；87%的链中超过93%的突变重复了之前见过的结构形式，变异主要限于模板内的词元替换；短循环和自循环主导了转移结构；提示措辞和模型选择影响收敛速率，但现象本身具有鲁棒性；经典GP子树突变算子不出现类似收敛。

Conclusion: LLM驱动程序进化的核心存在一种张力：使其具备语义感知程序转换能力的同时，也带有系统性的结构同质性偏差，必须在设计开放探索系统时加以考虑。

Abstract: When an LLM repeatedly mutates a program, does it explore new forms or circle back to the same ones? We study this question by analyzing LLM-driven mutation chains in the absence of selection pressure within a domain-specific language, varying prompt design, model family, and stochastic replication. We find that LLM-based mutation consistently converges toward restricted attractor regions in program space. Convergence is especially severe at the structural level: in 87% of chains, over 93% of mutations revisit a previously seen structural form, with most variation confined to terminal substitutions within recurring templates. Cycle analysis reveals short cycles and self-loops dominating the transition structure. The rate of convergence varies with prompt wording and model choice, but the phenomenon is robust across conditions. A classical GP subtree mutation operator does not exhibit comparable convergence, suggesting that the effect is intrinsic to the LLM mutation pipeline. These findings reveal a tension at the heart of LLM-driven program evolution: the same capabilities that enable semantics-aware program transformation also carry a systematic bias toward structural homogeneity that must be accounted for if such systems are to sustain open-ended exploration. Source code is available at https://github.com/can-gurkan/lmca.

</details>


### [254] [A Motivational Architecture for Conversational AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05411)
*Anna Mikeda,Ben Goertzel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向对话智能体的动机架构，将OpenPsi动机理论与MetaMo高层动机支架相结合，重新定义了以对话为核心的内稳态机制，并提出了十阶段动机处理流水线、双轨决策策略、以及行动前情感与行动后情绪的区分，适用于伴侣型和研究型两种对话智能体案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知AI的动机架构主要为物理智能体设计，关注生理需求调节，而对话智能体的感知运动回路是语言性的，环境是用户不断演化的心理状态，因此需要一套以对话为本的动机体系来替代传统的生理内稳态模型。

Method: 提出三项核心贡献：(1) 十阶段动机处理流水线，将认知调节与情境评估在架构上分离；(2) 双轨决策策略，融合紧迫驱动的快速响应与深思熟虑的多目标优化；(3) 区分行动前情感（pre-action feelings）与行动后情绪（post-action emotions）作为功能不同的情感形式。将框架应用于CompanionAgent和ResearchAgent两个示例智能体。

Result: 构建了面向对话智能体的动机架构，以内稳态调节语言化动机（能力、不确定性降低、亲和、情感依恋、合法性、滋养、审美一致性）替代生理需求，通过模块化执行基座实现动机处理与决策。

Conclusion: 该框架可扩展至社交机器人和领域通用的人工通用智能（AGI），为对话智能体提供了一种非生理驱动的动机设计范式。

Abstract: Motivational architectures in cognitive AI have largely been designed for physical agents regulating bodily needs. Conversational agents operate in a different regime: their sensorimotor loop is linguistic, their environment is a user's evolving mental state, and their consequential actions are speech acts, tool invocations, and strategic silences. This paper proposes a conversational reinterpretation of the OpenPsi motivational lineage, coupled to MetaMo's higher-level motivational scaffold, for agents built on a modular execution substrate. Homeostasis is recast in dialogue-native terms: the agent regulates competence, uncertainty reduction, affiliation, affinity, legitimacy, nurturing, and aesthetic coherence rather than bodily deficits. We propose three contributions: a ten-stage motivational processing pipeline that architecturally separates cognitive modulation from situational appraisal; a dual decision strategy blending urgency-driven fast response with deliberative multi-goal optimization; and an architecturally useful distinction between pre-action feelings and post-action emotions as functionally different forms of affect. We specialize the framework to two example agents -- CompanionAgent and ResearchAgent -- and sketch its extension to social robotics and domain-generic human-level AGI.

</details>


### [255] [Assessing the Carbon Emissions and Energy Consumption of U.S. Hyperscale Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05420)
*Gianluca Guidi,Francesca Dominici,Tiziano Squartini,Callaway Sprinkle,Jonathan Gilmour,Kevin Butler,Eric Bell,Scott Delaney,Falco J. Bargagli-Stoffi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了2024年5月至2025年4月期间美国403个超大规模数据中心（HDC）的电力消耗、电力来源及CO2排放。在中心情景下，HDC消耗约68-99 TWh电力（约占全美用电量1.8%），其中约54%来自化石燃料，碳排放强度（545 gCO2/kWh）比全美电网平均值（370 gCO2/kWh）高出约48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI驱动的超大规模数据中心在美国快速扩张，其环境足迹引发关注。当前缺乏基于设施级最新数据的系统性评估工具，亟需量化此类设施的电力消耗与碳排放。

Method: 收集了2024年5月至2025年4月期间403个美国超大规模数据中心的设施级信息，利用EPA eGRID电厂级数据，估计了不同设施负荷情景下的电力消耗量、电力来源构成及归因CO2排放量，并计算了电力加权平均碳强度。

Result: 不同情景下HDC消耗约68-99 TWh电力，对应约37-54百万吨CO2排放。中心情景下，HDC占全美用电量约1.8%，约54%的归因发电来自化石燃料。HDC电力加权平均碳强度约545 gCO2/kWh，较同期全美电网平均碳强度（370 gCO2/kWh）高出约48%。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一个基于最新EPA eGRID电厂级数据的归因性评估工具，揭示了美国超大规模数据中心的电力消耗高度依赖化石燃料，且其碳强度显著高于全国电网平均水平。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of hyperscale data centers (HDCs) in the US, mainly driven by the adoption of artificial intelligence, has raised concerns about this industry's environmental footprint. We compiled facility-level information on 403 US hyperscale data centers operating between May 2024 and April 2025 and estimated their electricity consumption, electricity sources, and attributable CO2 emissions. Across different facility-load scenarios, these HDCs consumed approximately 68-99 TWh of electricity and were associated with about 37-54 million metric tons of CO2. Under the central scenario, HDC electricity demand corresponded to approximately 1.8% of total US electricity consumption, with roughly 54% of attributed generation supplied by fossil-fuel sources. The HDC electricity-weighted average carbon intensity was approximately 545 gCO2/kWh, about 48% above the contemporaneous US national grid-average carbon intensity of 370 gCO2/kWh. Our approach provides an attributional tool for assessing the environmental footprint of hyperscale data centers using the most recent EPA eGRID plant-level data.

</details>


### [256] [Minimizing the Hidden Cost of Scales: Graph-Guided Ultra-Low-Bit Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05429)
*Rayyan Abdalla,Amir Hussein,Min Wu,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAGE-PTQ框架，通过基于分布统计的权重显著性分离、稀疏图建模非显著权重的分组优化、双模量化（显著权重多比特+非显著权重二值化）以及自适应显著性阈值选择，在超低比特量化中实现极低缩放开销（平均1.03权重比特、0.004缩放比特/矩阵），显著优于BiLLM和PB-LLM等SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的超低比特PTQ方法依赖刚性的权重显著性假设或位置启发式，引入大量隐藏的缩放开销，亟需一种能最小化缩放成本的量化框架。

Method: 1) 基于分布统计分离显著权重与非显著权重；2) 将子采样的非显著权重建模为稀疏图以估计每层最优分组数；3) 对显著权重分配多比特精度，对非显著权重进行二值化；4) 显著权重使用每通道一个缩放因子，每组非显著权重使用单个标量缩放因子；5) 自适应显著性阈值选择以确定每矩阵最优显著性比率。

Result: 平均1.03权重比特和0.004缩放比特/矩阵；LLaMA-3-8B上WikiText2困惑度6.74（BiLLM为55.8），GPU内存低于BiLLM的50%；LLaMA-2-70B上在单张NVIDIA L40 GPU上实现1.5倍解码加速。

Conclusion: SAGE-PTQ通过显著性感知和图引导的方法，在超低比特量化中实现了极低的缩放开销，在困惑度、内存占用和推理速度上全面超越现有方法。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is critical for the efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs). Recent ultra-low-bit PTQ methods rely on rigid weight-saliency assumptions or position heuristics, introducing substantial hidden scaling overhead. We propose SAGE-PTQ (Saliency-Aware Graph-guided Efficient PTQ), a novel ultra-low-bit quantization framework for LLMs that minimizes hidden scaling cost. SAGE-PTQ separates salient and unsalient weights using distributional statistics, then models subsampled unsalient weights as a sparse graph to estimate the optimal number of groups per layer. SAGE-PTQ applies dual-mode quantization, assigning multi-bit precision to salient weights and binarizing unsalient weights. To reduce scaling overhead, SAGE-PTQ uses one per-channel scale for salient weights and one scalar per unsalient group. Finally, SAGE-PTQ implements adaptive saliency thresholding to select the optimal saliency ratio per matrix. SAGE-PTQ achieves 1.03 weight bits and only 0.004 scaling bits per matrix on average, outperforming state-of-the-art methods such as BiLLM and PB-LLM. On LLaMA-3-8B, SAGE-PTQ achieves 6.74 WikiText2 perplexity, compared to 55.8 for BiLLM, while using less than 50% of BiLLM's GPU memory. On LLaMA-2-70B, SAGE-PTQ provides 1.5x faster decoding on one NVIDIA L40 GPU, demonstrating practical inference efficiency.

</details>


### [257] [Zero knowledge verification for frontier AI training is possible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05433)
*Pierre Peigné,Ky Nguyen,Paul Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对前沿AI密集预训练的零知识验证架构，通过结合预提交训练规范、节点间网络观测、即时Merkle承诺和原生BF16/FP32预编译的zkVM，在约36个月内可实现部署概念验证，训练侧开销仅个位数百分比，将训练记录转化为可治理执行的工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿AI治理框架以累积训练算力作为高影响模型的主要判定标准，但执行依赖于自我报告，缺乏技术验证原语。任何未来国际协议面临同样问题，而历史上具有显著外部性的技术协调监管均依赖技术验证，否则协议仅具有声明性。近期治理分析认为零知识证明是有前景的候选方案，但在前沿规模下尚不实用。

Method: 提出一种验证架构，结合：(1)预提交训练规范，(2)节点间网络观测，(3)即时Merkle承诺记录中间计算，通过带原生BF16/FP32预编译的零知识虚拟机(zkVM)进行验证。证明检查GPU实际执行的浮点计算而非定点近似，通过私密训练规范保护模型架构机密性。协议产生三种证明类型：初始化时的创世证明、运行中的步骤证明、以及将策略相关约束作为运行不变量的事前认证。

Result: 估计部署概念验证约需36个月，训练侧开销为个位数百分比；验证级定制硅片周期为6-10年。整理出13个开放研究与工程问题作为外部贡献的研究议程。

Conclusion: 零知识证明在前沿AI训练验证中的不可行性是范式局限而非根本性障碍，所提架构可在合理时间范围内实现可部署的概念验证，为AI治理提供技术验证基础。

Abstract: Frontier AI governance frameworks increasingly use cumulative training compute as the primary criterion for designating high-impact models, but enforcement rests on self-reporting because no technical verification primitive for training exists. Any future international agreement on frontier AI faces the same problem at higher stakes: coordinated regulation of technologies with significant externalities has historically rested on technical verification, without which agreements are declaratory. Recent governance analyses judge zero-knowledge proofs a promising candidate but currently impractical at frontier scale [26, 4]. We argue the impracticality is paradigm-bound rather than fundamental, and propose a verification architecture for frontier dense pre-training combining a pre-committed training specification, inter-node network observations, and on-the-fly Merkle commitments of intermediate computation, verified through a zero-knowledge Virtual Machine (zkVM) with native BF16/FP32 precompiles. The proof checks the actual floating-point computation the GPU performed rather than a fixed-point approximation, and preserves model-architecture confidentiality through a private training specification. The protocol produces three proof types: a genesis proof at initialisation, in-training step proofs across the run, and ex-ante attestations enforcing policy-relevant claims as running invariants, turning the training record into a governance-enforceable artefact. We estimate a deployable proof of concept within approximately 36 months at single-digit-percent training-side overhead, against a six-to-ten-year cycle for verification-grade custom silicon. Thirteen open research and engineering problems are catalogued as a research agenda for external contribution

</details>


### [258] [Ten Headache Specialists versus Artificial Intelligence for Clinical Literature Summarization: A Critical Evaluation and Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05436)
*Alejandro Lozano,Keiko Ihara,Ping-Hao Yang,Carrie E. Robertson,Jennifer Stern,Allan Purdy,Hsiangkuo Yuan,Pengfei Zhang,Yulia Orlova,Olga Fermo,Jennifer Hranilovich,Fred Cohen,Todd J. Schwedt,Jenelle A. Jindal,Serena Yeung-Levy,Chia-Chun Chiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究构建了基于RAG的AI代理框架，利用三种大语言模型生成医学文献摘要，并与头痛专家的手工摘要进行盲法比较。专家撰写的摘要整体更受偏好，但专家有时难以区分人类与AI生成的摘要。研究还识别了专家重视的摘要特征，为未来文献摘要流程优化提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策依赖最新医学文献，但医生面临时间有限和文献量激增的挑战。尽管检索增强大语言模型在临床摘要任务中展现出潜力，但其在综合更广泛的科学文献方面的效果以及与专家撰写摘要的直接比较仍缺乏人工评估。

Method: 构建基于RAG的代理AI框架，使用Sonnet、GPT-4o和Llama 3.1三种LLM。头痛专家创建13个问题（3个用于提示优化，10个用于评估）。10位头痛专家各撰写一个问题摘要，每个问题获得4份摘要（专家、Sonnet、GPT-4o、Llama）。专家在盲法下根据正确性、完整性、简洁性和临床效用对摘要进行1-10分评分，并进行偏好排序和来源判断（人类或AI）。

Result: 专家撰写的摘要整体上更受偏好，但专家有时难以区分人类与AI生成的摘要。研究还识别了专家重视的关键特征，这些特征超出了标准评估指标。

Conclusion: 尽管当前LLM在医学文献摘要方面尚未超越专家水平，但已展现出竞争力。研究发现的专家重视特征可为未来人工和AI文献摘要流程的改进提供指导。

Abstract: Summarizing the latest medical literature to guide clinical decision-making is essential for evidence-based medicine and high-quality patient care. Yet clinicians face increasing challenges due to limited time with patients and a rapidly growing volume of published articles. Although retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in clinical summarization, human evaluations of their effectiveness in synthesizing broader scientific literature and direct comparisons to expert-written syntheses remain scarce. We constructed a RAG-based agentic AI framework using three state-of-the-art LLMs: Sonnet, GPT-4o, and Llama 3.1. A headache specialist created 13 questions, three for prompt optimization and ten for evaluation. Ten headache specialists across the United States and Canada each wrote a summary for one question, yielding four summaries per question (expert, Sonnet, GPT-4o, and Llama). The experts, blinded to authorship, critically evaluated the summaries, excluding the topic for which they wrote a summary, based on correctness, completeness, conciseness, and clinical utility, scoring each from 1 to 10 using standardized rubrics. They also ranked the summaries by preference and indicated whether they believed each summary was written by an expert or an LLM. Our study, comparing LLM- and expert-written literature summaries evaluated by headache specialists, showed that expert-written summaries were preferred, although experts sometimes found it challenging to distinguish between human- and AI-generated summaries. We also identified key expert-valued features beyond standard evaluation metrics that can guide future refinement of both human and AI literature summarization pipelines.

</details>


### [259] [Brick-Composer: Using MLLMs for Assembly with Diverse Bricks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05445)
*Jiateng Liu,Bingxuan Li,Zhenhailong Wang,Rushi Wang,Kaiwen Hong,Cheng Qian,Jiayu Liu,Denghui Zhang,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Manling Li,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BC-Bench基准测试，评估多模态大语言模型在积木组装任务中的视觉定位与空间推理能力，并设计Brick-Composer训练框架，通过人类设计火花、世界反馈和合成经验三种信号提升模型性能，使组装成功率从不足1%提升至约15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多模态大语言模型是否具备从任意设计图中读取信息并利用可复用积木构建真实世界物体的视觉定位与空间推理能力，推动AI代理向自主构建真实物体的愿景迈进。

Method: 将积木组装形式化为序列决策问题（积木选择+姿态估计）；构建BC-Bench基准测试；提出Brick-Composer框架，利用人类设计火花（提供丰富可供性构造演示）、世界反馈（将预测动作锚定在视觉与物理后果中）、合成经验（超越现有物体设计进行规模化学习）三种互补信号训练MLLM。

Result: Brick-Composer将积木选择准确率提升超3倍，显著降低姿态估计误差，严格步骤级组装成功率从不足1%提升至约15%；Qwen-3-8B模型训练后可正确完成完整物体中高达42%的组装步骤。

Conclusion: 当前最先进MLLM在精细积木选择和精确姿态估计上仍远未达到可靠建造者水平；通过针对性、物理接地学习，MLLM可以获取组装能力，表明该方向具有发展潜力。

Abstract: We dream of AI agents that can read arbitrary designs and construct real-world objects from reusable building blocks. As a first step toward this vision, we study whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) possess the visual grounding and spatial reasoning capabilities required for brick assembly. We formulate brick assembly as a sequential decision-making problem, where each step involves two subtasks: brick selection, identifying the target brick from candidate components, and brick pose estimation, predicting where and how the selected brick should be placed. To support this study, we introduce BC-Bench (Brick Construction Benchmark), the first benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on assembly with diverse bricks. Experiments show that current state-of-the-art MLLMs remain far from reliable builders, struggling with fine-grained brick selection and failing at precise pose estimation. To bridge this gap, we propose Brick-Composer, a learning framework that equips MLLMs with assembly skills through three complementary signals: Human Design Sparks, which provide affordance-rich construction demonstrations; World Feedback, which grounds predicted actions in visual and physical consequences; and Synthetic Experience, which scales learning beyond existing object designs. Brick-Composer improves brick selection accuracy by over three times, substantially reduces pose estimation errors, and raises strict step-level assembly success from less than 1% to around 15%. After training, a Qwen-3-8B can correctly compose up to 42% of the steps for a complete object, suggesting that MLLMs can acquire assembly capabilities through targeted, physically grounded learning.

</details>


### [260] [Insurance of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05449)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能体AI（能够自主规划、调用工具、执行决策并持续修改数字与物理环境的AI系统）带来的新兴保险市场，分析了其承保、定价、再保险及产品设计含义，提出了基于风险评估、情景分析、依赖映射和累积风险管理的精算框架，并构建了整合多种险种的协作保险架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统超越了传统信息生成范畴，具备自主规划、工具调用、决策执行及持久性环境修改能力，其带来的新型风险暴露无法被传统保险类别（如网络险、职业责任险、产品责任险、董责险）充分覆盖，因此需要研究针对智能体AI的新兴保险市场及其承保与定价框架。

Method: 将智能体AI刻画为自主性与授权委托程度的连续谱系，强调信息输出与能独立产生可保事件的外部行动之间的区别。分析主要风险路径（幻觉、提示注入攻击、自主决策错误、模型漂移、依赖故障、网络-物理危害），评估现有保险产品的适应性调整。提出基于暴露评估、情景分析、依赖映射和累积风险管理的精算框架，并借鉴网络保险的演进经验，构建整合多种险种的协作保险架构。

Result: 现有保险产品正在调整以应对智能体AI风险暴露；提出的精算框架可系统评估风险；构建的协作保险架构包含网络险、技术错误与遗漏险、产品责任险、性能保证险和主动AI责任险，通过明确的分配机制和专用AI聚合额度实现整合。

Conclusion: 智能体AI保险的未来不在于单一险种产品，而在于一个由互补险种构成的层次化生态系统，其发展有赖于治理改进、透明度提升、遥测技术和监管清晰度的增强。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) systems are transforming the risk landscape by extending beyond information generation to autonomous planning, tool invocation, decision execution, and persistent modification of digital and physical environments. These capabilities introduce novel exposures that do not fit neatly within traditional insurance categories such as cyber, professional liability, product liability, or directors and officers coverage. This paper examines the emerging insurance market for agentic AI and develops a framework for understanding its underwriting, pricing, reinsurance, and product-design implications. We characterize agentic AI as a continuum of autonomy and delegated authority, emphasizing the distinction between informational outputs and systems capable of independently generating insured events through external actions. We analyze major risk pathways, including hallucinations, prompt-injection attacks, autonomous decision errors, model drift, dependency failures, and cyber-physical harms, and evaluate how existing insurance products are adapting to address these exposures. The paper further proposes an actuarial framework based on exposure assessment, scenario analysis, dependency mapping, and accumulation-risk management, drawing parallels to the evolution of cyber insurance.
